# KSP parity ledger

Generated from `sampler_ksp::parity::ledger()` by `cargo run -p sampler-ksp --example parity_ledger`. Do not edit by hand.

Statuses describe the supported subset, not parsing success. Per-use lowering may worsen a status through the `@lower:*` rows. Opaque constants and unverified host/DSP bindings remain Approximate. Compile failures add `@syntax`; authored values and source text are never included. Historical corpus counts in KSP_COVERAGE.md and the manual inventory in KSP_SURFACE.json are not implementation status authorities.
